+export async function createFiniteCoupledOwnerWorld(){
+ const main=await open(process.env.AFW_C2_DROP_WINNER==='1');let active=main,pendingForm=null,firstCookie=null,negative=null;const observer=createFiniteSnapshotObserver(main,()=>active);
+ const isolate=async()=>{await close(negative);negative=await open();active=negative;return negative;};
+ const noFurther=async(f,before,response)=>{assert.notEqual(await discard(response),200);assert.deepEqual(await count(f),before);};
+ return {
+  async close(){await close(negative);await close(main);},
+  counts:observer.counts,
+  async approve(stage){
+   active=main;const f=main;
+   if(stage==='connection'){
+    const response=await approveFirstConnection(f);assert.equal(await discard(response),200);firstCookie=f.choice.cookie;
+   }else{
+    const form=pendingForm??await choose(f);pendingForm=null;
+    if(stage==='finite')assert(form.text.includes('revocar únicamente la concesión OAuth creada aquí.'));
+    assert.equal(await discard(await post(f,form)),200);f.choice=form;
+   }
+   const rows=await plain(f.stub.snapshot()),receipts=records(rows).filter(([k])=>k.endsWith(':receipt')).map(([,raw])=>JSON.parse(raw));
+   assert.equal(receipts.length,['connection','notices','finite'].indexOf(stage)+1);
+   const ordered=['connection','notices','finite'].slice(0,receipts.length).map(s=>receipts.find(r=>r.stage===s));assert(ordered.every(Boolean));
+   assert.equal(new Set(ordered.map(r=>r.versionId)).size,ordered.length);assert.equal(new Set(ordered.map(r=>r.deploymentId)).size,ordered.length);
+   for(let i=0;i<ordered.length;i++){
+    const r=ordered[i],previous=i?ordered[i-1]:null;
+    if(previous){assert.equal(r.baselineVersionId,previous.versionId);assert.equal(r.baselineDeploymentId,previous.deploymentId);}
+    for(const effect of ['upload','activation']){
+     const key='afw.finite-policy-effect.v1:'+identityHash({kind:'connection',preconnectionId:r.preconnectionId,sourceRevision:r.sourceRevision,stage:r.stage,effect});
+     const send=JSON.parse(rows.find(([k])=>k===key+':send')[1]),ack=JSON.parse(rows.find(([k])=>k===key+':ack')[1]);
+     assert.equal(ack.sendHash,hash('ConnectionSend',send));assert.equal(ack.resourceId,effect==='upload'?r.versionId:r.deploymentId);assert(ack.acknowledgedAt<send.deadline);
+    }
+    assert.equal(hash('ConnectionReceipt',r),r.receiptHash);
+   }
+   assert.equal(f.uploads.length-1,ordered.length);assert.equal(f.activations.length-1,ordered.length);
+   // Mount the actual provider-returned version/configuration, with unchanged actual compiled M bytes.
+   await f.adoptConnectionConfiguration({upstream:true});
+  },
+  async oauth(){

+/** Negative SDK-only writer. It deliberately omits AFW's callback; never an M admission. */
+export async function compileCleanupSdkOnlyWorker(){
+ const source=String.raw`
+ import {OAuthAuthorizationServer} from '@cloudflare/workers-oauth-provider';
+ import {createFiniteOwnerRequestLease,readFiniteOwnerRequestBoundary} from './lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-preparation.mjs';
+ const O='https://delegated-canary.agentfriendlyweb.dev',scopes=['afw:project:read'];
+ const server=new OAuthAuthorizationServer({issuer:O,resources:[O+'/mcp'],scopesSupported:scopes,accessTokenTTL:300,refreshTokenTTL:0,clientIdMetadataDocumentEnabled:false});
+ const hash=async v=>Buffer.from(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',new TextEncoder().encode(v))).toString('hex');
+ const check=(v,message)=>{if(!v)throw Error('SYN SDK-only '+message);};
+ let retained=null,release=null,signal={reached:false,settled:false};
+ export default{async fetch(request,env){
+  const path=new URL(request.url).pathname;
+  if(path==='/_fixture/sdk-only-signal')return Response.json(signal);
+  if(path==='/_fixture/sdk-only-release'){release?.();return new Response(null,{status:204});}
+  if(path!=='/_fixture/sdk-only-exchange'||request.method!=='POST')return new Response(null,{status:404});
+  if(retained)return new Response(null,{status:409});
+  const root=createFiniteOwnerRequestLease(request),boundary=readFiniteOwnerRequestBoundary(root.lease);
+  const startedMono=performance.now(),childDeadline=Math.min(boundary.deadline,boundary.startedAt+5000);
+  const current=()=>{root.lease.assertCurrent();check(Date.now()<childDeadline&&performance.now()-startedMono<5000,'original child expired');};
+  let timer,bodyFinish;
+  signal={label:'SYN adversarial SDK-only; AFW callback deliberately omitted',worker:'sdk-only-first-connection',callbackOmitted:true,MAdmission:false,rootStartedAt:boundary.startedAt,rootDeadline:boundary.deadline,childDeadline,reached:false,settled:false,retained:false,released:false,logicalDeniedAt:null,nativeReturned:false,nativeRejected:false};
+  try{
+   const input=await request.json();current();
+   check(Number.isSafeInteger(input.C0)&&Date.now()<input.C0,'original C0');
+   const original=server.getOAuthApi(env),summary=await original.unwrapToken(input.subjectToken);current();
+   check(summary&&summary.grantId===input.sdkGrantId&&summary.grant.clientId===input.clientId&&summary.audience===O+'/mcp'&&JSON.stringify(summary.scope)===JSON.stringify(scopes),'same genuine token identity/scope/audience');
+   check(summary.expiresAt*1000-Date.now()>=65000,'genuine token needs >=65s remaining');
+   const grantKey='grant:'+summary.userId+':'+summary.grantId,grant=await env.OAUTH_KV.get(grantKey,{type:'json'});current();
+   check(grant?.id===summary.grantId&&grant.metadata?.afwFiniteCleanup?.correlationHash===input.correlationHash,'same native grant/correlation');
+   signal.tokenHash=await hash(input.subjectToken);signal.selectorHash=await hash(summary.grantId);signal.subjectHash=await hash(summary.userId);signal.correlationHash=input.correlationHash;signal.referenceHash=input.referenceHash;signal.tokenExpiresAt=summary.expiresAt;signal.grantExpiresAt=grant.expiresAt??null;signal.C0=input.C0;
+   const kv=env.OAUTH_KV,prefix='token:'+summary.userId+':'+summary.grantId+':';
+   const wrapped={get(...args){current();return kv.get(...args);},list(){throw Error('SYN no SDK list');},delete(){throw Error('SYN no SDK delete');},async put(key,value,options){
+    current();check(!signal.reached&&key.startsWith(prefix),'single original token write only');
+    const native=JSON.parse(value);check(native.grantId===summary.grantId&&native.userId===summary.userId&&native.audience===summary.audience&&JSON.stringify(native.scope)===JSON.stringify(scopes),'same SDK write tuple');
+    signal.reached=true;signal.reachedAt=Date.now();signal.write={keyHash:await hash(key),valueHash:await hash(value),expiresAt:native.expiresAt,expirationTtl:options.expirationTtl};current();
+    release=()=>{signal.released=true;signal.releasedAt=Date.now();};
+    while(!signal.released){check(Date.now()<input.C0+180000,'original C1 retention observation exhausted');await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,250));}
+    // The already-started SDK operation survives logical denial. No new business work is admitted.
+    signal.nativeAttemptAt=Date.now();
+    try{const result=await kv.put(key,value,options);signal.nativeReturned=true;return result;}
+    catch(error){signal.nativeRejected=true;throw error;}
+    finally{signal.nativeSettledAt=Date.now();}
+   }};
+   current();
+   const actual=server.getOAuthApi({...env,OAUTH_KV:wrapped}).exchangeToken({subjectToken:input.subjectToken,scope:scopes,aud:O+'/mcp'});
+   retained=actual;signal.retained=true;
+   // Register settlement before the race; only this exact promise can clear retention.
+   actual.then(async value=>{signal.sdkReturned=true;signal.resultTokenHash=await hash(value.access_token);},()=>{signal.sdkRejected=true;}).finally(()=>{signal.settled=true;signal.settledAt=Date.now();signal.retained=false;retained=null;release=null;bodyFinish?.();}).catch(()=>{});
+   const denied=new Promise(resolve=>{timer=setTimeout(()=>resolve('deadline'),Math.max(0,childDeadline-Date.now()));});
+   const outcome=await Promise.race([actual.then(()=> 'early-return',()=> 'early-rejection'),denied]);
+   signal.logicalDeniedAt=Date.now();signal.logicalReason=outcome;root.finish();clearTimeout(timer);
+   // Fixed denied headers/body are available at the original child deadline. Keeping the
+   // transport body open retains execution context, not the expired logical Root.
+   const body=new ReadableStream({start(controller){controller.enqueue(new TextEncoder().encode('SYN SDK-only denied'));bodyFinish=()=>{try{controller.close();}catch{}};if(signal.settled)bodyFinish();},cancel(){return actual.catch(()=>{});}});
+   return new Response(body,{status:403,headers:{'Content-Type':'text/plain','Cache-Control':'no-store'}});
+  }catch{
+   root.finish();clearTimeout(timer);signal.logicalDeniedAt=Date.now();signal.logicalReason='premise-rejected';return new Response('SYN SDK-only premise rejected',{status:412});
+  }
+ }};
+`;
+ const result=await build({bundle:true,write:false,metafile:true,platform:'neutral',mainFields:['module','main'],format:'esm',target:'es2022',external:['node:*','cloudflare:*'],legalComments:'none',stdin:{contents:source,resolveDir:process.cwd()}});
+ return {script:result.outputFiles[0].text,bytes:result.outputFiles[0].contents.length,sha256:digest(result.outputFiles[0].contents),metafile:result.metafile,sourceKind:'SYN adversarial SDK-only; AFW callback omitted'};

+export function createDropDiagnostic(hash) {
+  const qaHeader = 'x-afw-lab-qa-denial', outerHeader = 'x-afw-lab-m-denial';
+  const requestEpoch = new WeakMap(), qaResults = new WeakMap();
+  const cells = new WeakMap(), leases = new WeakMap(), responses = new WeakMap(), deadlines = new WeakMap();
+  const outerReads = new WeakMap();
+  const seenQa = new WeakSet(), seenOuter = new WeakSet();
+  const hex = value => typeof value === 'string' && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(value);
+  const uuid = value => typeof value === 'string' && /^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[1-5][a-f0-9]{3}-[89ab][a-f0-9]{3}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/.test(value);
+  const join = id => uuid(id) ? hash('afw.lab.drop.uuid.v2\0' + id) : null;
+  const tag = value => typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 0 && value.length <= 128 ? hash('afw.lab.drop.form.v2\0'+value) : null;
+  const unknown = () => ({case:'15:firstDROP',site:'UNKNOWN',outer:'UNKNOWN',publicSite:'UNKNOWN'});
+  function guard(fn) { return (...args) => { try {return fn(...args);} catch {return null;} }; }
+  function writeOnce(r, name, value) {
+    if (!(r instanceof Response) || r.ok || r.status < 400 || r.status > 599 || r.headers.has(name)) return false;
+    r.headers.set(name,value);
+    return r.headers.get(name) === value;
+  }
+  function frame(line) {
+    if (typeof line !== 'string' || line.length > 768) return null;
+    const match = /^\s+at (?:[^()\n]* \()?([^ ()\n]+):([1-9][0-9]{0,7}):([1-9][0-9]{0,7})\)?$/.exec(line);
+    return match && {script:match[1],line:Number(match[2]),column:Number(match[3])};
+  }
+  function arm(scriptKey) {return {scriptKey,live:true,sealed:false,poisoned:false,request:null,entryJoin:null,formTag:null,qaStatus:0,qaEgress:false,coords:null};}
+  function enter(e,r,id,handle) {
+    if (!e || !e.live || e.sealed) return;
+    if (e.request || requestEpoch.has(r)) {e.poisoned=true;return;}
+    e.request=r;e.entryJoin=join(id);e.formTag=tag(handle);
+    if (!e.entryJoin || !e.formTag) e.poisoned=true;
+    requestEpoch.set(r,e);
+  }
+  function register(r,q,frames) {
+    const e=requestEpoch.get(r);
+    if (!e?.live || e.sealed || e.poisoned) return;
+    if (e.q || !(q instanceof Response) || q.ok || seenQa.has(q) || qaResults.has(q)) {e.poisoned=true;return;}
+    const first=frame(frames?.[0]), next=frame(frames?.[1]);
+    e.q=q;qaResults.set(q,{e,r,status:q.status,coords:first && next && first.script===next.script && first.script===e.scriptKey ? [first,next] : null});
+  }
+  function qaEnd(e,r,q) {
+    if (!e?.live || e.sealed || e.poisoned || !q) return;
+    const item=qaResults.get(q);
+    if (!item || item.e!==e || item.r!==r || e.request!==r || item.status!==q.status || seenQa.has(q)) {e.poisoned=true;return;}
+    seenQa.add(q);
+    if (!writeOnce(q,qaHeader,'q1.'+e.entryJoin+'.'+q.status)) {e.poisoned=true;return;}
+    e.qaEgress=true;e.qaStatus=q.status;e.coords=item.coords;
+  }
+  function cut(e) {
+    if (!e || !e.live || e.sealed) return null;
+    e.sealed=true;
+    return {entryJoin:e.entryJoin,formTag:e.formTag,qaEgress:e.qaEgress,qaStatus:e.qaStatus,coords:e.coords,poisoned:e.poisoned};
+  }
+  function cell(p) {
+    if (cells.has(p)) {cells.get(p).bad=true;return null;}
+    const c={p,lease:null,j:null,bad:false,d:null,status:0};cells.set(p,c);return c;
+  }
+  function bind(p,l) {
+    const c=cells.get(p);
+    if (!c) return;
+    if (c.lease || leases.has(l)) {c.bad=true;return;}
+    c.lease=l;leases.set(l,c);
+  }
+  function assign(p,id) {
+    const c=cells.get(p);
+    if (!c) return;
+    if (c.j || !c.lease) {c.bad=true;return;}
+    c.j=join(id);
+  }
+  function denied(p,l,r,d) {
+    const c=cells.get(p);
+    if (!c || c.bad || leases.get(l)!==c || c.lease!==l || !c.j || c.d || !(r instanceof Response) || r.ok || !d) return;
+    c.d=d;
+    if (r.headers.get(qaHeader)==='q1.'+c.j+'.'+r.status) c.status=r.status;
+  }
+  function deadline(l,error) {
+    const c=leases.get(l);
+    if (c && c.lease===l && !c.bad && error) deadlines.set(error,c);
+  }
+  function caught(p,l,error,b) {
+    const c=cells.get(p);
+    if (!c || c.bad || !hex(c.j) || leases.get(l)!==c || c.lease!==l || c.b || responses.has(b) || seenOuter.has(b)) return;
+    const winner=error && error===c.d ? (c.status?'V':'S') : error && deadlines.get(error)===c ? 'L' : 'U';
+    c.b=b;responses.set(b,{c,p,winner,inner:winner==='V'?c.status:0});
+  }
+  function outerEnd(c,p,b) {
+    const x=responses.get(b);
+    if (!x || !c || c.bad || x.c!==c || x.p!==p || cells.get(p)!==c || seenOuter.has(b)) return;
+    seenOuter.add(b);
+    writeOnce(b,outerHeader,'o1.'+c.j+'.'+b.status+'.'+x.winner+'.'+x.inner);
+  }
+  function tap(c,p,actual) {
+    if (!c || !(actual instanceof Promise)) return;
+    actual.then(value=>{try{outerEnd(c,p,value);}catch{}},()=>{}).catch(()=>{});
+  }
+  function receive(r) {
+    if (r instanceof Response && !outerReads.has(r)) outerReads.set(r,{status:r.status,ok:r.ok,marker:r.headers.get(outerHeader)});
+  }
+  function host(response,state,handle,unique,mapped,qaTransport,mTransport) {
+    const out=unknown();
+    const received=outerReads.get(response);
+    if (!received || !state || state.poisoned || !unique || !mTransport || state.formTag!==tag(handle) || !hex(state.entryJoin) || received.ok) return out;

+function dropCachedPlugin(original, data) {
+  const loads=new Map(),resolves=new Map();
+  return {name:'drop-single-preparation',setup(builder){
+    const wrap=(cache,fn,key)=>args=>{
+      const k=key(args);
+      if (!cache.has(k)) cache.set(k,Promise.resolve().then(()=>fn(args)));
+      return cache.get(k);
+    };
+    const loadKey=a=>JSON.stringify([a.namespace,a.path,a.suffix,a.with]);
+    const resolveKey=a=>JSON.stringify([a.namespace,a.importer,a.path,a.resolveDir,a.kind,a.with]);
+    const registerLoad=(opt,fn)=>{
+      const cache=new Map(); // callback index/order is shared by both builds below
+      const i=counter++; if(!loads.has(i))loads.set(i,cache);
+      builder.onLoad(opt,wrap(loads.get(i),async a=>{
+        const r=await fn(a);if(r?.contents!==undefined)data.set(a.path,Buffer.from(r.contents).toString('utf8'));return r;
+      },loadKey));
+    };
+    let counter=0;
+    original.setup({...builder,onLoad:registerLoad,onResolve(opt,fn){
+      const i=counter++;if(!resolves.has(i))resolves.set(i,new Map());
+      builder.onResolve(opt,wrap(resolves.get(i),fn,resolveKey));
+    }});
+    registerLoad({filter:/.*/,namespace:'file'},async a=>{
+      const ext=a.path.split('.').pop(),loader=({js:'js',mjs:'js',cjs:'js',ts:'ts',mts:'ts',cts:'ts',json:'json',jsx:'jsx',tsx:'tsx'})[ext];
+      if(!loader)throw Error('DROP_MAP_UNSUPPORTED_LOADER');
+      return {contents:await readFile(a.path),loader,resolveDir:a.path.slice(0,a.path.lastIndexOf('/'))};
+    });
+    const index=counter++;if(!resolves.has(index))resolves.set(index,new Map());
+    const fallback=wrap(resolves.get(index),async a=>{
+      const r=await builder.resolve(a.path,{kind:a.kind,resolveDir:a.resolveDir,importer:a.importer,namespace:a.namespace,pluginData:'drop-resolve-default',with:a.with});
+      return {path:r.path,external:r.external,namespace:r.namespace,suffix:r.suffix,sideEffects:r.sideEffects,errors:r.errors,warnings:r.warnings};
+    },resolveKey);
+    builder.onResolve({filter:/.*/},a=>a.pluginData==='drop-resolve-default'?undefined:fallback(a));
+  }};
+}

+function createDropSiteMap(map,prepared,js) {
+  const unknown='UNKNOWN', chars='ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/';
+  const specs=[
+    ['S01:69','lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-preparation.mjs',"if(closed||controller.signal.aborted||!Number.isSafeInteger(at)||at<last||at>=deadline||elapsed<0||elapsed>=budget)fail();last=at;",'Finite owner preparation unavailable'],
+    ['S01:95','lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-preparation.mjs',"if(!active||controller.signal.aborted||at<last||at>=end||elapsed<0||elapsed>=end-wall)fail();",'Finite owner preparation unavailable'],
+    ['S01:247','lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-preparation.mjs',"if(used?.status!=='recorded')fail();",'Finite owner preparation unavailable'],
+    ['S00:743','lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-phase-context.mjs','if(!reservation||!rootRow||!grantRow||!prep.noticeLink||!expected.issuerRows.grantLink)fail();','Finite owner phase context unavailable'],
+    ['S04:37','lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-cleanup-scope.mjs','if(old!==undefined)fail();','Finite owner cleanup scope unavailable'],
+    ['S04:43','lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-cleanup-scope.mjs','if(!observed||canonical(observed)!==canonical(value))fail();','Finite owner cleanup scope unavailable']
+  ];
+  const refs=[];
+  for(const [label,path,guard,message] of specs){
+    const source=prepared.get(process.cwd()+'/'+path),si=map.sources.indexOf(path);
+    if(typeof source!=='string'||si<0||map.sources.lastIndexOf(path)!==si)continue;
+    const lines=source.split('\n');
+    for(const [role,needle,token] of [['throw',"Error('"+message+"')",'Error'],['call',guard,'fail']]){
+      const rows=lines.map((s,i)=>s.includes(needle)?i:-1).filter(n=>n>=0);
+      if(rows.length!==1)continue;
+      const row=rows[0],start=lines[row].indexOf(needle)+(role==='throw'?0:guard.lastIndexOf('fail()'));
+      refs.push({label,role,si,row,start,token,points:[]});
+    }
+  }
+  const lines=js.split('\n');let si=0,sr=0,sc=0;
+  const groups=map.mappings.split(';');
+  for(let row=0;row<groups.length;row++){
+    let col=0;
+    for(const segment of groups[row].split(',')){
+      if(!segment)continue;
+      const nums=[];let bits=0,shift=0;
+      for(const ch of segment){const digit=chars.indexOf(ch);if(digit<0||shift>30)throw Error('DROP_MAP_INVALID');
+        bits+=(digit&31)*2**shift;if(digit&32){shift+=5;}else{nums.push((bits&1?-1:1)*Math.floor(bits/2));bits=0;shift=0;}}
+      if(shift||![1,4,5].includes(nums.length))throw Error('DROP_MAP_INVALID');
+      col+=nums[0];if(nums.length===1)continue;
+      si+=nums[1];sr+=nums[2];sc+=nums[3];
+      for(const ref of refs){
+        if(si!==ref.si||sr!==ref.row||sc!==ref.start)continue;
+        const tail=lines[row]?.slice(col)??'';
+        if(!(ref.token==='Error'?/^Error\s*\(/:/^fail[0-9]*\s*\(/).test(tail))continue;
+        ref.points.push({line:row+1,column:col+1});
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  const sites=[];
+  for(const [label] of specs){
+    const t=refs.find(r=>r.label===label&&r.role==='throw'),c=refs.find(r=>r.label===label&&r.role==='call');
+    if(t?.points.length===1 && c?.points.length===1)sites.push({label,throw:t.points[0],call:c.points[0]});
+  }
+  const usable=sites.filter(s=>sites.filter(t=>J([t.throw,t.call])===J([s.throw,s.call])).length===1);
+  return {usable,lookup(coords){
+    if(!Array.isArray(coords)||coords.length!==2||!coords[0]?.script||coords[0].script!==coords[1]?.script)return unknown;
+    const equal=(p,q)=>p.line===q.line&&p.column===q.column;
+    return usable.find(s=>equal(s.throw,coords[0])&&equal(s.call,coords[1]))?.label??unknown;
+  }};
+}
+async function pairedDropBuild(options,data) {
+  const primary=await build(options),file='afw-c2-private-map.js';
+  try{const sibling=await build({...options,outfile:file,sourcemap:'external',sourcesContent:false});

+  const map=JSON.parse(external.text);assert(map.version===3 && !Object.hasOwn(map,'sourcesContent'));
+  return {primary,sites:createDropSiteMap(map,data,code.text),qaHash:sha(code.contents),sameBytes:true};
+  }catch{return {primary,sites:{usable:[]},qaHash:sha(primary.outputFiles[0].contents),sameBytes:false};}

+export function resolveDropSite(binding,coords) {
+  if (!binding || binding.prep!==binding.siblingPrep || !binding.realNamespace ||
+      binding.qa!==binding.sibling || binding.qa!==binding.mountedQA || binding.m!==binding.mountedM ||
+      !Array.isArray(binding.sites) || binding.sites.length<2 || !Array.isArray(coords) || coords.length!==2 ||
+      !coords[0]?.script || coords[0].script!==coords[1]?.script) return 'UNKNOWN';
+  const at=(ref,p)=>ref.line===p.line && ref.column===p.column;
+  const matches=binding.sites.filter(s=>at(s.throw,coords[0])&&at(s.call,coords[1]));
+  return matches.length===1?matches[0].label:'UNKNOWN';
+}
+export function dropTicket(rows,form,decodeTicket) {
+  try {
+    const key=sha(form.handle);
+    if(!Array.isArray(rows)||rows.length>512||rows.some(r=>!Array.isArray(r)||r.length!==2))return false;
+    const matching=rows.filter(r=>r[0]==='owner-ticket:'+key);
+    if(matching.length!==1)return false;
+    const t=decodeTicket('Ticket',matching[0][1]);
+    return t.action==='finite' && t.projectRef===form.projectRef && t.ticketHash===key;
+  } catch {return false;}

+const family = 'afw.c2-drop-winner-diag4-';
+export const markers = Object.freeze({
+  custody: 'AFW_C2_DROP_WINNER_DIAG4_CUSTODY',
+  outcome: 'AFW_C2_DROP_WINNER_DIAG4_OUTCOME',
+  fault: 'AFW_C2_DROP_WINNER_DIAG4_FAULT'

+export const flags = Object.freeze({
+  historicalCauseProven: false, receiptAuthenticated: false, operationalReadiness: false,
+  completePackage: false, PCoff: false, globalNativeDrainVerified: false, sdkNamespaceEmptyVerified: false

+export function requireFact(condition, code) {
+  if (!condition) throw Object.assign(new Error(code), { code });
+}
+export const same = (left, right) => JSON.stringify(left) === JSON.stringify(right);
+export const sha = bytes => createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
+export function hashes(bytes) {
+  return { bytes: bytes.length, sha256: sha(bytes),
+    git: createHash('sha1').update(Buffer.from('blob ' + bytes.length + '\0')).update(bytes).digest('hex') };
+}
+export function match(bytes, pin, code) {
+  const actual = hashes(bytes);
+  requireFact(actual.bytes === pin.bytes && actual.git === (pin.blob ?? pin.git)
+    && actual.sha256 === pin.sha256, code);
+  return actual;
+}
+const numberIn = (n, lo, hi) => Number.isSafeInteger(n) && n >= lo && n <= hi;
+const sameInode = (a,b) => a.dev === b.dev && a.ino === b.ino;
+const stable = (a,b) => sameInode(a,b) && a.size === b.size &&
+  a.mtimeMs === b.mtimeMs && a.ctimeMs === b.ctimeMs;
+const privateFile = v => v.isFile() && v.nlink === 1 && (v.mode & 0o777) === 0o600 &&
+  v.uid === process.getuid() && v.gid === process.getgid();
+
+// Read the actual inode and check its path again after reading; a replaced/torn file
+// is different from a stable file ending in a partial journal record.
+async function measuredRead(path, limit, code, identity = null, privateMode = false) {
+  let handle;
+  try {
+    requireFact(await realpath(path) === resolve(path), code);
+    handle = await open(path, constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW | constants.O_NONBLOCK);
+    const before = await handle.stat();
+    requireFact(before.isFile() && before.nlink === 1 && numberIn(before.size,0,limit)
+      && (!identity || sameInode(identity,before)) && (!privateMode || privateFile(before)), code);
+    const data = Buffer.alloc(before.size);
+    let offset = 0;
+    while (offset < data.length) {
+      const {bytesRead} = await handle.read(data,offset,data.length-offset,offset);
+      requireFact(bytesRead > 0,code);
+      offset += bytesRead;
+    }
+    const after = await handle.stat(), named = await lstat(path);
+    requireFact(stable(before,after) && stable(after,named) && (!privateMode || privateFile(named)),code);
+    return data;
+  } catch { throw Object.assign(new Error(code), {code}); }
+  finally { if (handle) await handle.close(); }
+}
+export async function measureTargets(root, entries) {
+  requireFact(entries.length === 13 && new Set(entries.map(e=>e.path)).size === 13,'TARGET_DRIFT');
+  const observed = [];
+  for (const entry of entries) {
+    const bytes = await measuredRead(join(root,entry.path),entry.after.bytes,'TARGET_DRIFT');
+    observed.push(match(bytes,entry.after,'TARGET_DRIFT').sha256);
+  }
+  return observed;
+}
+export async function verifySiblings(root, selection) {
+  const spec = selection.registrationTail, d = selection.driver, s = selection.originalDriver;
+  const original = await measuredRead(join(root,s.path),s.bytes,'TARGET_DRIFT');
+  const driver = await measuredRead(join(root,d.path),d.bytes,'TARGET_DRIFT');
+  const recorder = await measuredRead(join(root,selection.recorder.path),selection.recorder.bytes,'TARGET_DRIFT');
+  match(original,s,'TARGET_DRIFT'); match(driver,d,'TARGET_DRIFT'); match(recorder,selection.recorder,'TARGET_DRIFT');
+  const a=original.subarray(spec.originalOffset), b=driver.subarray(spec.driverOffset);
+  requireFact(d.path !== s.path && a.length === spec.bytes && a.equals(b) && sha(a) === spec.sha256,'TARGET_DRIFT');
+  const alias=selection.aliases.filter(x=>x.id==='D07');
+  requireFact(alias.length===1 && alias[0].path===d.path && alias[0].blob===d.git,'TARGET_DRIFT');
+  return spec.sha256;
+}
+export async function installSiblings(root,selection,driver,recorder) {
+  match(driver,selection.driver,'TARGET_DRIFT'); match(recorder,selection.recorder,'TARGET_DRIFT');
+  requireFact(selection.driver.path!==selection.originalDriver.path,'TARGET_DRIFT');
+  await writeFile(join(root,selection.driver.path),driver,{flag:'wx',mode:0o600});
+  await writeFile(join(root,selection.recorder.path),recorder,{flag:'wx',mode:0o600});
+  return verifySiblings(root,selection);
+}
+export async function createJournal(path) {
+  const h=await open(path,constants.O_CREAT|constants.O_EXCL|constants.O_WRONLY|constants.O_NOFOLLOW,0o600);
+  try {
+    const s=await h.stat();
+    requireFact(privateFile(s)&&s.size===0,'JOURNAL_INVALID');
+    return s;
+  } finally { await h.close(); }
+}
+export const readJournal = (path,initial) => measuredRead(path,32768,'JOURNAL_INVALID',initial,true);
+export async function readProbe(path) {
+  const raw=await measuredRead(path,1024,'CUSTODY_INVALID',null,true);
+  const expected={schema:'afw.c2-oauth-custody.v1',uidMatches:true,gidMatches:true,groupsCleared:true,loopbackOnly:true};
+  requireFact(raw.equals(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(expected)+'\n')),'CUSTODY_INVALID');
+  return sha(raw);

+export function freshProbes(before,after) {
+  const valid=names=>Array.isArray(names)&&names.length<=8&&new Set(names).size===names.length&&
+    names.every(n=>/^[1-9][0-9]*\.json$/.test(n));
+  requireFact(valid(before)&&valid(after)&&before.every(n=>after.includes(n)),'CUSTODY_INVALID');
+  const count=after.filter(n=>!before.includes(n)).length;
+  requireFact(count>=1 && count<=7,'CUSTODY_INVALID');
+  return count;
+}
+export function custodyFrame(context,preflightProcesses,greenProcesses,checks) {
+  const count=preflightProcesses+greenProcesses;
+  requireFact(numberIn(preflightProcesses,1,7)&&numberIn(greenProcesses,1,7)&&numberIn(count,2,8),'CUSTODY_INVALID');
+  for (const key of ['parentNonRoot','preflightOwned','preflightRemoved','uidGidGroupsLoopbackMatched',
+    'targetsBeforeMatched','targetsAfterMatched','journalOwnershipMatched','ownRootVerified','ownTempRemoved'])
+    requireFact(checks[key]===true,'CUSTODY_INVALID');
+  return {schema:family+'custody.v1',context,parentNonRoot:checks.parentNonRoot,
+    preflightOwned:checks.preflightOwned,preflightRemoved:checks.preflightRemoved,checkedLaunches:2,
+    preflightProcesses,greenProcesses,checkedProcesses:count,uidGidGroupsLoopbackMatched:checks.uidGidGroupsLoopbackMatched,
+    targetsBeforeMatched:checks.targetsBeforeMatched,targetsAfterMatched:checks.targetsAfterMatched,
+    journalOwnershipMatched:checks.journalOwnershipMatched,ownRootVerified:checks.ownRootVerified,ownTempRemoved:checks.ownTempRemoved};
+}
+const targetId = 'C2T1_NOTICES', projectionSchema = 'afw.c2-first-failure-post-diag2.v1';
+const sourceKeys = ['id','status','phase','counters','code'], counterKeys = ['upload','activation','receipts'];
+const keyOrder = (o, keys) => o !== null && typeof o === 'object' && !Array.isArray(o) && same(Object.keys(o),keys);
+const emptyProjection = (state,reason) => ({schema:projectionSchema,state,reason,throwSequence:null,source:null,assertion:null});
+const unknown = reason => emptyProjection('UNKNOWN',reason);
+const outside = reason => emptyProjection('NOT_APPLICABLE',reason);
+const targetThrow = e => e?.id===targetId && e.operation==='action' && e.edge==='throw' &&
+  e.error?.name==='AssertionError' && e.error.code==='ERR_ASSERTION' &&
+  same(e.error.site,{file:'S08',line:41,column:12});
+const prevPhases = ['connection','oauth',...Array(9).fill('negative'),'notices'];
+
+// C2SOURCE and the flat TAP result stream have separate ordering. Never take a
+// console row inside diagnostic text, or an assertion from another result block.
+function targetFromWire(lines,ids) {
+  let diagnostic=false, owner=null, targetBlocks=0, fields=null, closed=false, layout=false, duplicate=false;
+  let next=0, lastRow=-1, targetRow=null, sourceError=null;
+  const seenRows=new Set();
+  for(const line of lines) {
+    if(line==='  ---') {
+      if(diagnostic || owner===null) layout=true;
+      if(owner===targetId) { if(++targetBlocks!==1) duplicate=true; fields=new Map(); }
+      diagnostic=true;
+      continue;
+    }
+    if(line==='  ...') {
+      if(!diagnostic) layout=true;
+      if(diagnostic && owner===targetId) closed=true;
+      diagnostic=false;
+      continue;
+    }
+    if(diagnostic) {
+      if(!line.startsWith('    ') && line!=='') {
+        if(!line.startsWith('  ')) {layout=true;continue;}
+        if(owner===targetId) {
+          const m=/^  ([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*):(?: (.*))?$/.exec(line);
+          if(!m) {layout=true;continue;}
+          const [,key,value]=m;
+          if(fields.has(key)) duplicate=true;
+          fields.set(key,value??'');
+        }
+      }
+      continue;
+    }
+    if(line.startsWith('# Subtest: ')) {owner=null;continue;}
+    const pair=/^(?:not )?ok [1-9]\d* - (C2T1_[A-Z_]+)$/.exec(line);
+    if(pair) {owner=pair[1];continue;}
+    if(!line.startsWith('# C2SOURCE ')) continue;
+    const encoded=line.slice(11);
+    let row;
+    try {
+      row=JSON.parse(encoded);
+      if(!keyOrder(row,sourceKeys)||!keyOrder(row.counters,counterKeys)||JSON.stringify(row)!==encoded) throw 0;
+    } catch {sourceError='AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE';continue;}
+    const pos=ids.indexOf(row.id);
+    if(pos<0||seenRows.has(row.id)||pos<=lastRow||(pos<=11 && pos!==next)) {
+      sourceError='AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE';
+    }
+    seenRows.add(row.id); lastRow=pos;
+    if(pos<=11 && pos>=0) {
+      next=pos+1;
+      if(pos===11) targetRow=row;
+      if(sourceError!=='AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE') {
+        const passed=pos!==11;
+        if(row.status!==(passed?'pass':'fail') || row.code!==(passed?'OK':'TEST_FAILED') || row.phase!==prevPhases[pos])
+          sourceError='CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE';
+        else if(!sourceError && !counterKeys.every(k=>numberIn(row.counters[k],0,3))) sourceError='UNSUPPORTED_SCALARS';
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  if(diagnostic && owner===targetId) return unknown('TRUNCATED_NATIVE');
+  if(layout) return unknown('NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED');
+  if(sourceError) return unknown(sourceError);
+  if(!targetRow) return unknown('SOURCE_ROW_UNAVAILABLE');
+  if(duplicate) return unknown('AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE');
+  if(!fields || !closed) return unknown('ASSERTION_SCALARS_UNAVAILABLE');
+  const tags={failureType:"'testCodeFailure'",code:"'ERR_ASSERTION'",name:"'AssertionError'",operator:"'strictEqual'"};
+  for(const [key,expected] of Object.entries(tags)) {
+    if(!fields.has(key)) return unknown('NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED');
+    if(fields.get(key)!==expected) return unknown('CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE');
+  }
+  if(!fields.has('actual')||!fields.has('expected')) return unknown('ASSERTION_SCALARS_UNAVAILABLE');
+  // Only calibrated top-level scalar fields. Nested map/text content is opaque.
+  if(fields.get('type')!=="'test'" || !/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(fields.get('duration_ms')??'') ||
+     !fields.has('location') || fields.get('error')!=='|-' || fields.get('stack')!=='|-' ||
+     [...fields.keys()].some(k=>!['duration_ms','type','location','failureType','error','code','name',
+       'expected','actual','operator','stack'].includes(k))) return unknown('NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED');
+  const [actual,expected]=['actual','expected'].map(key=>{
+    const scalar=fields.get(key);
+    return /^(?:0|[1-9]\d*)$/.test(scalar) ? Number(scalar) : NaN;
+  });
+  if(!numberIn(actual,100,599)||!Number.isSafeInteger(expected)) return unknown('UNSUPPORTED_SCALARS');
+  if(actual===200||expected!==200) return unknown('CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE');
+  return {source:targetRow,assertion:{actual,expected}};
+}
+
+// Only called with the validated monotonic prefix from read-progress. The
+// projector never reconstructs a missing operation or reads any external data.
+export function projectFirstFailure(stdout,facts,ids) {
+  const {witness,native,firstThrowSequence}=facts, events=witness.events, first=events.find(e=>e.edge==='throw');
+  requireFact((first?.sequence??null)===firstThrowSequence && ids.length===20 && ids[11]===targetId,'EVIDENCE_CONFLICT');
+  const settled=(id,op,edge)=>events.some(e=>e.id===id&&e.operation===op&&e.edge===edge);
+  if(first && !targetThrow(first)) return outside('EARLIEST_THROW_DIFFERENT');
+  const earlier=native.cases.slice(0,11), target=native.cases[11];
+  if(earlier.some(e=>e.status==='fail')) return outside('EARLIER_NATIVE_FAILURE');
+  if(target?.id===targetId && target.status==='pass')
+    return settled(targetId,'action','return') && settled(targetId,'count','return') ?
+      outside('TARGET_PASSED') : unknown('JOURNAL_UNRESOLVED');
+  if(!first) return witness.integrity==='complete' ? outside('NO_QUALIFYING_THROW') : unknown('JOURNAL_UNRESOLVED');
+  if(!ids.slice(0,11).every(id=>settled(id,'action','return')&&settled(id,'count','return')))
+    return unknown('JOURNAL_UNRESOLVED');
+  if(!settled(targetId,'count','enter')||!settled(targetId,'count','return')) return unknown('COUNTER_UNAVAILABLE');
+  if(native.integrity==='torn') return unknown('TRUNCATED_NATIVE');
+  let text;
+  try {text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true,ignoreBOM:true}).decode(stdout);}
+  catch {return unknown('NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED');}
+  if(/[\r\t\0]/.test(text) || (text!==''&&!text.startsWith('TAP version 13\n'))) return unknown('NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED');
+  if(native.integrity==='ambiguous') return unknown('AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE');
+  if(earlier.length!==11 || !target || target.id!==targetId || target.status!=='fail') return unknown('TARGET_NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE');
+  const parsed=targetFromWire(text.split('\n'),ids);
+  if(parsed.state) return parsed;
+  return {schema:projectionSchema,state:'KNOWN',reason:'TARGET_POST_ASSERTION',throwSequence:first.sequence,
+    source:parsed.source,assertion:parsed.assertion};
+}
+
+const finiteSchema='afw.c2-finite-get-diag3.v1';
+const monitorId='C2T1_MONITOR', scopeId='C2T1_SCOPE_MISSING';
+const fullPhases=[...prevPhases,'monitor','monitor','scope','finite','selection','negative','negative','negative'];
+const bucketValues=['0','1','2+','UNKNOWN'];
+const missedHttp=(reason,state='UNKNOWN')=>({state,reason,throwSequence:null,actual:null,expected:null});
+const missedCount=reason=>({value:'UNKNOWN',reason,countSequence:null});
+const scopeThrow=e=>e?.id===scopeId&&e.operation==='action'&&e.edge==='throw'&&e.error?.name==='AssertionError'&&
+ e.error.code==='ERR_ASSERTION'&&same(e.error.site,{file:'S08',line:18,column:118});
+
+function finiteJournal(facts,ids,ordinal) {
+ const events=facts.witness.events;
+ const id=ids[ordinal-1],prior=ids.slice(0,ordinal-1);
+ const settled=(key,op,edge)=>events.find(e=>e.id===key&&e.operation===op&&e.edge===edge);
+ const priorReturned=prior.every(k=>settled(k,'action','return')&&settled(k,'count','return'));
+ const returned=settled(id,'count','return'),action=settled(id,'action','return')??settled(id,'action','throw');
+ const joined=priorReturned&&returned&&action&&(ordinal!==14||action.edge==='return')&&
+   !events.some(e=>e.id==='AFTER'&&e.sequence<returned.sequence);
+ let reason=null;
+ if(!joined)reason='JOURNAL_UNRESOLVED';
+ else if(facts.native.integrity!=='prefix'||facts.native.cases.length<ordinal||
+   !prior.every((k,i)=>facts.native.cases[i]?.id===k&&facts.native.cases[i].status==='pass')||
+   facts.native.cases[ordinal-1]?.id!==id||
+   facts.native.cases[ordinal-1]?.status!==(action.edge==='return'?'pass':'fail'))reason='NATIVE_UNJOINED';
+ return {reason,returned,priorReturned,action};
+}
+
+function finiteCandidate(facts,ids,join) {
+ const first=facts.witness.events.find(e=>e.edge==='throw'),native=facts.native;
+ if(first&&!scopeThrow(first))return missedHttp('EARLIEST_THROW_DIFFERENT','NOT_APPLICABLE');
+ if(native.cases.slice(0,14).some(r=>r.status==='fail'))return missedHttp('EARLIER_NATIVE_FAILURE','NOT_APPLICABLE');
+ if(native.cases[14]?.status==='pass')return join.action?.edge==='return'&&join.returned?
+  missedHttp('TARGET_PASSED','NOT_APPLICABLE'):missedHttp('JOURNAL_UNRESOLVED');
+ if(!first)return facts.witness.integrity==='complete'?missedHttp('NO_QUALIFYING_THROW','NOT_APPLICABLE'):missedHttp('JOURNAL_UNRESOLVED');
+ if(!join.priorReturned)return missedHttp('JOURNAL_UNRESOLVED');
+ if(!join.returned)return missedHttp('COUNTER_UNAVAILABLE');
+ if(native.integrity==='torn')return missedHttp('TRUNCATED_NATIVE');
+ if(native.integrity==='ambiguous')return missedHttp('AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE');
+ if(native.integrity!=='prefix'||native.cases[14]?.id!==ids[14]||native.cases[14]?.status!=='fail')return missedHttp('TARGET_NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE');
+ return null;
+}

+function scanFiniteWire(stdout,ids,native) {
+ const errors=[],markers=new Map(),rows=new Map();
+ let last=-1,markerLast=0,markerCount=0,invalidMarker=false;
+ let diag=false,owner=null,blockCount=0,fields=null,closed=false,duplicate=false,layout=false;
+ const sourceError=(index,reason)=>errors.push({index,reason});
+ let text;
+ try{text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true,ignoreBOM:true}).decode(stdout);}catch{}
+ if(typeof text!=='string'||(text!==''&&!text.startsWith('TAP version 13\n'))||/[\t\r\0]/.test(text)){
+  return {unsupportedEnvelope:true,rowError:()=> 'NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED',invalidMarker:true,markers,assertionError:'NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED'};
+ }
+ for(const line of text.split('\n')){
+  if(line==='  ---'){
+   if(diag||!owner)layout=true;
+   if(owner===scopeId){if(++blockCount!==1)duplicate=true;fields=new Map();}
+   diag=true;continue;
+  }
+  if(line==='  ...'){if(!diag)layout=true;if(owner===scopeId)closed=true;diag=false;continue;}
+  if(diag){
+   if(line&&!line.startsWith('    ')){
+    if(!line.startsWith('  ')){layout=true;continue;}
+    if(owner===scopeId){
+     const m=/^  ([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*):(?: (.*))?$/.exec(line);
+     if(!m){layout=true;continue;}
+     if(fields.has(m[1]))duplicate=true;
+     fields.set(m[1],m[2]??'');
+    }
+   }
+   continue;
+  }
+  if(line.startsWith('# Subtest: ')){owner=null;continue;}
+  const pair=/^(?:not )?ok [1-9]\d* - (C2T1_[A-Z_]+)$/.exec(line);
+  if(pair){owner=pair[1];continue;}
+  if(line.startsWith('# C2FT3')){
+   try{
+    if(++markerCount>2||!line.startsWith('# C2FT3 ')||Buffer.byteLength(line)+1>96)throw 0;
+    const body=line.slice(8),m=JSON.parse(body);
+    if(!keyOrder(m,['id','value'])||JSON.stringify(m)!==body||!bucketValues.includes(m.value))throw 0;
+    const ordinal=m.id===monitorId?14:m.id===scopeId?15:0;
+    if(!ordinal||markers.has(ordinal)||ordinal<=markerLast||last!==ordinal-2)throw 0;
+    markers.set(ordinal,m.value);markerLast=ordinal;
+   }catch{invalidMarker=true;}
+   continue;
+  }
+  if(!line.startsWith('# C2SOURCE '))continue;
+  const body=line.slice(11);
+  let r;
+  try{r=JSON.parse(body);}catch{sourceError(0,'AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE');continue;}
+  const pos=ids.indexOf(r?.id);
+  if(pos<0){sourceError(0,'AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE');continue;}
+  if(rows.has(pos)||pos<=last)sourceError(pos,'AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE');
+  if(pos>last+1)sourceError(last+1,'AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE');
+  last=pos;
+  rows.set(pos,r);
+  if(!keyOrder(r,sourceKeys)||!keyOrder(r.counters,counterKeys)||JSON.stringify(r)!==body)
+   sourceError(pos,'AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE');
+  else if(r.phase!==fullPhases[pos]||!['pass','fail'].includes(r.status)||r.code!==(r.status==='pass'?'OK':'TEST_FAILED')||
+    (native.cases[pos]&&r.status!==native.cases[pos].status))sourceError(pos,'CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE');
+  else if(!counterKeys.every(k=>numberIn(r.counters[k],0,3)))sourceError(pos,'UNSUPPORTED_SCALARS');
+ }
+ for(const ordinal of markers.keys())if(!rows.has(ordinal-1))invalidMarker=true;
+ const rowError=n=>{
+  const within=errors.filter(e=>e.index<n);
+  for(const reason of ['AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE','CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE','UNSUPPORTED_SCALARS'])
+   if(within.some(e=>e.reason===reason))return reason;
+  return rows.has(n-1)?null:'SOURCE_ROW_UNAVAILABLE';
+ };
+ const assertionError=diag&&owner===scopeId?'TRUNCATED_NATIVE':layout?'NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED':null;
+ return {rowError,markers,invalidMarker,assertionError,fields,closed,duplicate};
+}
+
+function finiteAssertion(scan,sequence) {
+ if(scan.assertionError)return missedHttp(scan.assertionError);
+ const sourceError=scan.rowError(15);
+ if(sourceError)return missedHttp(sourceError);
+ const {duplicate,fields,closed}=scan;
+  if(duplicate) return missedHttp('AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE');
+  if(!fields || !closed) return missedHttp('ASSERTION_SCALARS_UNAVAILABLE');
+  const tags={failureType:"'testCodeFailure'",code:"'ERR_ASSERTION'",name:"'AssertionError'",operator:"'strictEqual'"};
+  for(const [key,expected] of Object.entries(tags)) {
+    if(!fields.has(key)) return missedHttp('NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED');
+    if(fields.get(key)!==expected) return missedHttp('CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE');
+  }
+  if(!fields.has('actual')||!fields.has('expected')) return missedHttp('ASSERTION_SCALARS_UNAVAILABLE');
+  // Only calibrated top-level scalar fields. Nested map/text content is opaque.
+  if(fields.get('type')!=="'test'" || !/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(fields.get('duration_ms')??'') ||
+     !fields.has('location') || fields.get('error')!=='|-' || fields.get('stack')!=='|-' ||
+     [...fields.keys()].some(k=>!['duration_ms','type','location','failureType','error','code','name',
+       'expected','actual','operator','stack'].includes(k))) return missedHttp('NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED');
+  const [actual,expected]=['actual','expected'].map(key=>{
+    const scalar=fields.get(key);
+    return /^(?:0|[1-9]\d*)$/.test(scalar) ? Number(scalar) : NaN;
+  });
+  if(!numberIn(actual,100,599)||!Number.isSafeInteger(expected)) return missedHttp('UNSUPPORTED_SCALARS');
+  if(actual===200||expected!==200) return missedHttp('CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE');
+ return {state:'KNOWN',reason:'TARGET_GET_ASSERTION',throwSequence:sequence,actual,expected};
+}
+
+function finiteSample(join,scan,ordinal){
+ if(join.reason)return missedCount(join.reason);
+ if(scan.rowError(ordinal))return missedCount('SOURCE_UNJOINED');
+ if(scan.invalidMarker)return missedCount('MARKER_STREAM_INVALID');
+ if(!scan.markers.has(ordinal))return missedCount('MARKER_UNAVAILABLE');
+ const value=scan.markers.get(ordinal);
+ return value==='UNKNOWN'?missedCount('SNAPSHOT_INVALID'):{value,reason:'JOINED_SNAPSHOT',countSequence:join.returned.sequence};
+}
+
+function rawDependentExclusion(projection){
+ return projection?.state==='UNKNOWN'&&['AMBIGUOUS_EVIDENCE','TARGET_NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE'].includes(projection.reason);
+}

+function projectFiniteGet(stdout,facts,ids) {
+ requireFact(ids.length===20&&ids[13]===monitorId&&ids[14]===scopeId,'EVIDENCE_CONFLICT');
+ const monitor=finiteJournal(facts,ids,14),scope=finiteJournal(facts,ids,15);
+ const outside=finiteCandidate(facts,ids,scope);
+ const needsEnvelope=rawDependentExclusion(outside);
+ const scan=outside&&!needsEnvelope&&monitor.reason&&scope.reason?null:scanFiniteWire(stdout,ids,facts.native);
+ const http=needsEnvelope&&scan.unsupportedEnvelope?missedHttp('NATIVE_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED'):outside??finiteAssertion(scan,facts.firstThrowSequence);
+ return {schema:finiteSchema,http,
+  afterMonitor:finiteSample(monitor,scan,14),afterScopeMissing:finiteSample(scope,scan,15)};

+const dropUnknown=()=>({case:'15:firstDROP',site:'UNKNOWN',outer:'UNKNOWN',publicSite:'UNKNOWN'});
+function validDrop(d){
+  const sites=["S01:247","S00:743","S04:37","S04:43","S01:69","S01:95","UNKNOWN"],outer=["QA_MARKED_STUB_NON_OK","STUB_NON_OK","DEADLINE_OR_ABORT","UNKNOWN"];

+    (d.publicSite==='UNKNOWN'||d.publicSite===d.site&&d.outer==='QA_MARKED_STUB_NON_OK');

+function dropEligibility(facts,ids){
+  const w=facts.witness,n=facts.native;
+  if(facts.unknownReason!==null||!['prefix','complete'].includes(w.integrity)||n.integrity!=='prefix'||n.cases.length<14)return null;
+  const prior=ids.slice(0,14);
+  for(const [i,id] of prior.entries()){
+    if(n.cases[i].id!==id||n.cases[i].status!=='pass')return null;
+    for(const [j,[operation,edge]] of [['action','enter'],['action','return'],['count','enter'],['count','return']].entries()){
+      const e=w.events[4*i+j];
+      if(!e||e.id!==id||e.operation!==operation||e.edge!==edge||e.error!==null)return null;
+    }
+  }
+  const entered=w.events[56];
+  if(!entered||entered.id!==ids[14]||entered.operation!=='action'||entered.edge!=='enter'||entered.error!==null)return null;
+  if(!finiteJournal(facts,ids,15).reason)return 'closed15';
+  if(w.integrity==='prefix'&&w.events.length===57&&!w.afterOpen&&w.openCase?.id===ids[14]&&w.openCase.operation==='action'&&n.cases.length===14)return 'open15';
+  return null;
+}
+function projectDrop(stdout,facts,ids){
+  const un=dropUnknown(),window=dropEligibility(facts,ids);
+  if(!window)return un;
+  let text;try{text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(stdout);}catch{return un;}
+  const lines=text.split('\n'),matches=lines.filter(x=>x.includes('C2DW4'));
+  if(matches.length!==1||!matches[0].startsWith('# C2DW4 ')||!text.includes(matches[0]+'\n'))return un;
+  const scan=scanFiniteWire(stdout,ids,facts.native);if(scan.rowError(window==='closed15'?15:14))return un;
+  const at=lines.indexOf(matches[0]),before=lines.findIndex(x=>x.startsWith('# C2SOURCE {"id":"'+ids[13]+'",')),after=lines.findIndex(x=>x.startsWith('# C2SOURCE {"id":"'+ids[14]+'",'));
+  if(before<0||before>=at)return un;
+  if(window==='closed15'&&at>=after)return un;
+  if(window==='open15'&&lines.slice(before+1).some(x=>x.startsWith('# C2SOURCE')))return un;
+  const payload=matches[0].slice(8);
+  if(Buffer.byteLength(payload)>512)return un;
+  try{const d=JSON.parse(payload);return JSON.stringify(d)===payload&&validDrop(d)?d:un;}catch{return un;}
+}

+const stream = b=>({bytes:b.length,sha256:sha(b)});
+export function collectObservation(child,raw,ids) {
+  requireFact(Buffer.isBuffer(raw)&&raw.length<=32768,'JOURNAL_INVALID');
+  requireFact(Buffer.isBuffer(child.stdout)&&child.stdout.length<=1048576&&
+    Buffer.isBuffer(child.stderr)&&child.stderr.length<=65536,'CAPTURE_INVALID');
+  const timeout=child.error?.code==='ETIMEDOUT';
+  requireFact(timeout||(!child.error&&child.signal===null&&[0,1].includes(child.status)),'CAPTURE_INVALID');
+  const known=['SIGTERM','SIGKILL','SIGABRT','SIGSEGV','SIGINT','SIGHUP','SIGPIPE'];
+  const signal=child.signal===null?'none':known.includes(child.signal)?child.signal:
+    typeof child.signal==='string'?'other':'unavailable';
+  const capture={exitCode:numberIn(child.status,0,255)?child.status:null,signal,
+    errorKind:timeout?'timeout':'none',stdout:stream(child.stdout),stderr:stream(child.stderr)};
+  const fault=[child.stdout,child.stderr].some(b=>/^(?:# )?AFW_C2_GREEN_RECORDER_FAULT$/m.test(b.toString('utf8')));
+  const journal={...stream(raw),stable:true,recorderFaultObserved:fault};
+  const witness=progress(raw,ids,timeout?'timeout':'normal',fault);
+  const native=nativePrefix(child.stdout,ids);
+  const reason=fault?'RECORDER_FAULT':({empty:'JOURNAL_EMPTY',torn:'JOURNAL_TORN',ambiguous:'JOURNAL_INVALID_RECORD'}[witness.integrity]??null);
+  requireFact(!native.cases.some(n=>n.status==='pass' && witness.events.some(e=>
+    e.id===n.id && (e.operation==='action'||e.operation==='count') && e.edge==='throw')),'EVIDENCE_CONFLICT');
+  const facts={journal,witness,unknownReason:reason,
+    firstThrowSequence:witness.events.find(e=>e.edge==='throw')?.sequence??null,native};
+  return {capture,...facts,firstFailure:projectFirstFailure(child.stdout,facts,ids),finiteGet:projectFiniteGet(child.stdout,facts,ids),dropWinner:projectDrop(child.stdout,facts,ids)};

+function checkFinitePublication(o){
+ const f=o.finiteGet,ok=x=>requireFact(x,'PUBLICATION_INVALID');

+ }

+ // The sample reasons describe a Source-row gate that precedes marker parsing.
+ function prefixClaim(sample) {
+  if(sample.reason==='SOURCE_UNJOINED')return false;
+  if(['JOINED_SNAPSHOT','MARKER_STREAM_INVALID','MARKER_UNAVAILABLE','SNAPSHOT_INVALID'].includes(sample.reason))return true;
+  return null;
+ }
+ const sample14=prefixClaim(f.afterMonitor),sample15=prefixClaim(f.afterScopeMissing);
+ let http15=null;
+ if(h.state==='KNOWN'||h.reason==='ASSERTION_SCALARS_UNAVAILABLE')http15=true;
+ else if(h.reason==='SOURCE_ROW_UNAVAILABLE')http15=false;
+ ok(http15===null||sample15===null||http15===sample15);
+ if(http15===true||sample15===true)ok(sample14!==false);
+ encodeBody(f,512);
+}

+function publication(context,o) {
+  requireFact(Object.entries(fixedPublication).every(([k,v])=>context[k]===v),'PUBLICATION_INVALID');
+  requireFact(keyOrder(o,['capture','journal','witness','unknownReason','firstThrowSequence','native','firstFailure','finiteGet','dropWinner']),'PUBLICATION_INVALID');
+  const f=o.firstFailure;
+  requireFact(keyOrder(f,['schema','state','reason','throwSequence','source','assertion']) &&
+    f.schema===projectionSchema,'PUBLICATION_INVALID');
+  if(f.state==='KNOWN') {
+    const r=f.source,a=f.assertion;
+    requireFact(f.reason==='TARGET_POST_ASSERTION' && numberIn(f.throwSequence,1,128) &&
+      o.firstThrowSequence===f.throwSequence && targetThrow(o.witness.events.find(e=>e.edge==='throw')) &&
+      keyOrder(r,sourceKeys) && r.id===targetId && r.status==='fail' && r.phase==='notices' && r.code==='TEST_FAILED' &&
+      keyOrder(r.counters,counterKeys) && counterKeys.every(k=>numberIn(r.counters[k],0,3)) &&
+      keyOrder(a,['actual','expected']) && a.expected===200 && numberIn(a.actual,100,599) && a.actual!==200,
+      'PUBLICATION_INVALID');
+  } else {
+    const reasons=f.state==='UNKNOWN'?allowedReasons[0]:f.state==='NOT_APPLICABLE'?allowedReasons[1]:[];
+    requireFact(reasons.includes(f.reason) && f.throwSequence===null && f.source===null && f.assertion===null,'PUBLICATION_INVALID');
+  }
+  encodeBody(f,512);
+  checkFinitePublication(o);
+  requireFact(validDrop(o.dropWinner)&&(dropEligibility(o,selectionIds)!==null||same(o.dropWinner,dropUnknown())),'PUBLICATION_INVALID');
+  encodeBody(o.dropWinner,512);
 }
 export function successPacket(context,custody,beforeSha256,afterSha256,expected,observation) {
   publication(context,observation);
   requireFact(same(expected,targetHashes)&&same(beforeSha256,expected)&&same(afterSha256,expected),'TARGET_DRIFT');
   requireFact(same(context,custody.context),'CUSTODY_INVALID');
   const body={schema:family+'outcome.v1',context,diagnosticCollectionComplete:true,nativeQualification:false,
     sourceReads:{beforeSha256,afterSha256},observation,custody,SourceQualified:false,Task1Pass:false,nonclaims:flags};
   const c=encodeBody(custody,1024), o=encodeBody(body,24576);
   return {custodyLine:markers.custody+' '+c+'\n',outcomeLine:markers.outcome+' '+o+'\n',payload:o+'\n'};
 }

+function wireResponse(i,hop,r,name) {

+function blockHeaders(r,kind,name){

+   const original=r.headers;

+     if(k==='set')return ()=>{throw TypeError(kind);};
+     const x=Reflect.get(h,k,h);return typeof x==='function'?x.bind(h):x;

+export async function exercise(i){

+ if(i.hostCut==='absent')state=null;
+ if(i.hostCut==='foreign-join'&&state)state={...state,entryJoin:sha(i.foreignUuid)};
+ const form={handle:i.hostCut==='wrong-form'?'other-handle':i.handle,projectRef:i.project};
+ const t={action:'finite',projectRef:i.hostCut==='wrong-project-holder'?'foreign-project':i.project,ticketHash:sha(i.handle)};
+ const row=['owner-ticket:'+t.ticketHash,JSON.stringify(t)];
+ const unique=dropTicket(i.hostCut==='two-matching-tickets'?[row,row]:[row],form,(_,raw)=>JSON.parse(raw));
+ const prep={},binding={prep,siblingPrep:i.compiler==='equal-js-other-preparation'?{}:prep,
+   realNamespace:i.compiler!=='budget-compile-only',qa:'a',sibling:i.compiler==='unequal-generated-js'?'b':'a',
+   mountedQA:i.compiler==='other-mounted-script'?'other':'a',m:'m',mountedM:'m',sites:i.compiler==='only-one-distinct-anchor'?anchors.slice(0,1):anchors};
+ const mapped=resolveDropSite(binding,state?.coords);
+ const result=host.host(atHost,state,form.handle,unique,mapped,i.qaTransport!=='unverified',i.mHop!=='fallback-copy-all-fields-unverified')??host.unknown();
+ let originalAssertionPropagated=false;
+ if(i.originalPutsAssertion==='throw-after-cut'){
+   try{assert.equal(0,1,'controlled puts');}catch(error){originalAssertionPropagated=error instanceof assert.AssertionError && error.actual===0 && error.expected===1;}
+ }
+ if(ownedDeadline&&i.qaSettlement==='after-cut')settleQa();
+ clear.resolve();await actual.catch(()=>{});

+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import * as prev from './old-adapter.mjs';
+import * as next from './new-adapter.mjs';
+const allUnknown={case:'15:firstDROP',site:'UNKNOWN',outer:'UNKNOWN',publicSite:'UNKNOWN'};
+const joined={case:'15:firstDROP',site:'S04:43',outer:'QA_MARKED_STUB_NON_OK',publicSite:'S04:43'};
+export function differential(ids){
+ const logs=['TAP version 13'],events=[];
+ for(const [i,id] of ids.slice(0,15).entries()){
+   const phase=['connection','oauth',...Array(9).fill('negative'),'notices','monitor','monitor','scope'][i];
+   if(i===14)logs.push('MODEL_MARKER_SLOT');
+   logs.push('# C2SOURCE '+JSON.stringify({id,status:'pass',phase,counters:{upload:1,activation:1,receipts:1},code:'OK'}));
+   logs.push('# Subtest: '+id,'ok '+(i+1)+' - '+id);
+   for(const op of ['action','count'])for(const edge of ['enter','return'])events.push({sequence:events.length+1,elapsedMs:events.length,id,operation:op,edge,error:null});
+ }
+ const raw=Buffer.from(events.map(x=>JSON.stringify(x)+'\n').join(''));
+ const marker='# C2DW4 '+JSON.stringify(joined);
+ const tests=[
+   [[],raw,allUnknown],[[marker],raw,joined],[[marker,marker],raw,allUnknown],
+   [['# C2DW4 {"case":"private"}'],raw,allUnknown],[[marker],Buffer.alloc(0),allUnknown],
+   [[marker],Buffer.concat([raw,Buffer.from('{')]),allUnknown],
+   [['# C2DW4 '+JSON.stringify({...joined,publicSite:'S01:69'})],raw,allUnknown],
+   [['# C2DW4 '+JSON.stringify({...joined,outer:'STUB_NON_OK'})],raw,allUnknown],
+   [['# C2DW4 '+JSON.stringify({...joined,raw:'sample'})],raw,allUnknown],
+   [['# C2DW4 '+JSON.stringify({...joined,site:'S01:69',publicSite:'S01:69'})],raw,{...joined,site:'S01:69',publicSite:'S01:69'}]
+ ];
+ for(const [lines,journal,expected] of tests){
+   const child={stdout:Buffer.from([...logs.flatMap(l=>l==='MODEL_MARKER_SLOT'?lines:[l]),''].join('\n')),stderr:Buffer.alloc(0),status:0,signal:null};
+   const old=prev.collectObservation(child,journal,ids),{dropWinner,...rest}=next.collectObservation(child,journal,ids);
+   assert.deepStrictEqual(rest,old);assert.deepStrictEqual(dropWinner,expected);
+ }
+ const openLines=logs.slice(0,logs.indexOf('MODEL_MARKER_SLOT'));
+ const openRaw=Buffer.from(events.slice(0,57).map(x=>JSON.stringify(x)+'\n').join(''));
+ const variations=[[openLines,openRaw,'\n',joined],[openLines,openRaw,'\n#torn',allUnknown],
+   [openLines,Buffer.from(events.slice(0,56).map(x=>JSON.stringify(x)+'\n').join('')),'\n',allUnknown],
+   [openLines.filter(line=>!line.startsWith('# C2SOURCE {"id":"'+ids[13]+'",')),openRaw,'\n',allUnknown]];
+ for(const [lines,journal,end,want] of variations){
+   const child={stdout:Buffer.from([...lines,marker].join('\n')+end),stderr:Buffer.alloc(0),status:null,signal:'SIGTERM',error:{code:'ETIMEDOUT'}};
+   const old=prev.collectObservation(child,journal,ids),{dropWinner,...rest}=next.collectObservation(child,journal,ids);
+   assert.deepStrictEqual(rest,old);assert.deepStrictEqual(dropWinner,want);
+ }
+ return tests.length+variations.length;
+}

+    "sha256": "c21d600503bee20abfacc36779c846a34e46023d921ef6d6ceb697abcef50ebf"
+  },
+  "preflight": {
+    "path": "docs/superpowers/source-lab/c2-t1/c2-source-custody-preflight.mjs",
+    "blob": "d311f579e95ab64a5f2d897d6c40f1fce784e8e2",
+    "bytes": 417,
+    "sha256": "3e548a2dc9527e72997bf147dedcab1b8c34f49848ece68cff498991f200a9ae"
+  }
+};
+const flags={"historicalCauseProven":false,"receiptAuthenticated":false,"operationalReadiness":false,"completePackage":false,"PCoff":false,"globalNativeDrainVerified":false,"sdkNamespaceEmptyVerified":false};
+
+const workspace=resolve(process.cwd()), node=process.execPath, uid=process.getuid(),gid=process.getgid();
+const root=resolve(process.env.RUNNER_TEMP??''), parentEnv={PATH:dirname(node)+':/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin',HOME:root,LANG:'C.UTF-8',LC_ALL:'C.UTF-8'};
+let phase='admission', context=null, fault=null, temp=null, tempInitial=null, target=null, adapters=null;
+let beforeHashes, afterHashes, expected, observation, pfCount, greenCount;
+const valid={admission:['ADMISSION_INVALID'],materialize:['MATERIALIZATION_INVALID','TARGET_DRIFT'],
+  preflight:['PREFLIGHT_INVALID','CUSTODY_INVALID'],green:['LAUNCH_INVALID','CUSTODY_INVALID','CAPTURE_INVALID'],
+  journal:['JOURNAL_INVALID','RECORDER_FAULT','TARGET_DRIFT'],native:['CAPTURE_INVALID','EVIDENCE_CONFLICT'],
+  cleanup:['CLEANUP_INVALID','TARGET_DRIFT'],publish:['PUBLICATION_INVALID']};
+const fail=(reason=valid[phase][0])=>{throw Object.assign(new Error(reason),{code:reason});};
+const need=(condition,reason)=>{if(!condition)fail(reason);};
+const remain=(limit)=>{const ms=end-Date.now();need(ms>0);return Math.max(1,Math.min(ms,limit));};
+const digest=b=>createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
+const gitBlob=b=>createHash('sha1').update(Buffer.from('blob '+b.length+'\0')).update(b).digest('hex');
+const existsAbsent=async p=>{try{await lstat(p);}catch(e){if(e.code==='ENOENT')return;throw e;}fail();};
+async function pinned(rootDir,pin) {
+  const path=join(rootDir,pin.path);need((await lstat(path)).isFile() && await realpath(path)===resolve(path));
+  const data=await readFile(path);
+  need(data.length===pin.bytes&&gitBlob(data)===pin.blob&&digest(data)===pin.sha256);
+  return data;
+}
+function hostCmd(cmd,args,max=1048576,input) {
+  const result=spawnSync(cmd,args,{cwd:workspace,env:parentEnv,encoding:null,
+    input,stdio:['pipe','pipe','pipe'],maxBuffer:max,timeout:remain(cmd==='tar'?30000:330000)});
+  need(!result.error&&result.signal===null&&result.status===0&&Buffer.isBuffer(result.stdout)&&
+    result.stdout.length<=max&&Buffer.isBuffer(result.stderr)&&result.stderr.length<=65536);
+  return result.stdout;
+}
+const gitText=(...args)=>new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(hostCmd('git',args)).trim();
+async function materialize(index,files) {
+  target=join(temp,'proposal-green'); await mkdir(target,{mode:0o700});
+  const archive=hostCmd('git',['archive','--format=tar',config.BASE,'--','lib','worker','scripts','test','db','config',
+    config.bootstrap.path,...config.rootFiles],33554432);
+  hostCmd('tar',['-xf','-','-C',target],65536,archive);
+  await pinned(target,config.bootstrap);
+  for(const e of index.entries) {
+    const destination=join(target,e.path), admitted=await pinned(workspace,{...e.after,path:e.dataPath});
+    if(e.before) {
+      const old=await readFile(destination);
+      need((await lstat(destination)).isFile()&&old.length===e.before.bytes&&gitBlob(old)===e.before.blob);
+    } else await existsAbsent(destination);
+    await mkdir(dirname(destination),{recursive:true});
+    await writeFile(destination,admitted,{flag:e.before?'w':'wx'});
+  }
+  await symlink(join(workspace,'node_modules'),join(target,'node_modules'),'dir');
+  await writeFile(join(target,'c2-source-custody-guard.mjs'),files.guard,{flag:'wx'});
+  await writeFile(join(target,'c2-source-custody-preflight.mjs'),files.preflight,{flag:'wx'});
+}
+let qaTransport='unverified',mTransport='unverified';
+async function admitTransport(){
+  const pin=config.transportRelease;
+  if(!pin)return;
+  need(pin.path===config.prefix+'native-release.json');
+  const receipt=JSON.parse(await pinned(workspace,pin));
+  need(adapters.same(Object.keys(receipt),['schema','sourceIndex','fixture','toolHead','toolProof','independentReview','qaClosed','mClosed']));
+  need(receipt.schema==='AFW.C2.DROP_NATIVE_RELEASE/1'&&receipt.sourceIndex===pins.index.blob&&receipt.fixture==='a78efb5290deb184c70fc4282bf438d818d02588'&&
+    [receipt.toolHead,receipt.toolProof,receipt.independentReview].every(v=>/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(v))&&typeof receipt.qaClosed==='boolean'&&typeof receipt.mClosed==='boolean');
+  qaTransport=receipt.qaClosed?'verified':'unverified';mTransport=receipt.mClosed?'verified':'unverified';
+}
+let previous = new Map();
+async function probeMap() {
+  const dir=join(target,'custody-probes');
+  let names;
+  try{const stat=await lstat(dir);need(stat.isDirectory()&&stat.uid===uid&&stat.gid===gid,'CUSTODY_INVALID');names=await readdir(dir);}
+  catch(e){if(e.code==='ENOENT')return new Map();throw e;}
+  need(names.length<=8 && names.every(n=>/^[1-9][0-9]*\.json$/.test(n)),'CUSTODY_INVALID');
+  const map=new Map();
+  for(const name of names.sort()) {
+    const path=join(dir,name), stat=await lstat(path), h=await adapters.readProbe(path);
+    const last=await lstat(path);
+    need(stat.dev===last.dev&&stat.ino===last.ino&&stat.size===last.size&&stat.ctimeMs===last.ctimeMs,'CUSTODY_INVALID');
+    map.set(name,[last.dev,last.ino,h]);
+  }
+  return map;
+}
+async function checkedLaunch(args,limit) {
+  const prior=await probeMap();
+  need(adapters.same([...prior],[...previous]),'CUSTODY_INVALID');
+  const env={PATH:parentEnv.PATH,HOME:temp,TMPDIR:temp,RUNNER_TEMP:temp,XDG_CACHE_HOME:join(temp,'cache'),
+    XDG_CONFIG_HOME:join(temp,'config'),LANG:'C.UTF-8',LC_ALL:'C.UTF-8',CI:'true',NODE_ENV:'test',
+    AFW_C2_DROP_WINNER:'1',AFW_C2_QA_TRANSPORT:qaTransport,AFW_C2_M_TRANSPORT:mTransport,
+    GITHUB_SHA:context.head,GITHUB_REPOSITORY:config.repo,AFW_C2_DIAG_ROOT:target,
+    AFW_C2_DIAG_PARENT_UID:String(uid),AFW_C2_DIAG_PARENT_GID:String(gid)};
+  const child=spawnSync('sudo',['-n','/usr/bin/env','-i',...Object.entries(env).map(([k,v])=>k+'='+v),
+    'unshare','--net','/bin/sh','-c','uid="$1"; gid="$2"; shift 2; ip link set lo up && exec /usr/bin/setpriv --reuid "$uid" --regid "$gid" --clear-groups --no-new-privs "$@"',
+    'c2-source',String(uid),String(gid),node,'--import',join(target,'c2-source-custody-guard.mjs'),...args],
+    {cwd:target,env,encoding:null,maxBuffer:1048576,timeout:remain(limit),stdio:['ignore','pipe','pipe']});
+  const after=await probeMap();
+  for(const [key,v] of prior)need(adapters.same(after.get(key),v),'CUSTODY_INVALID');
+  const fresh=adapters.freshProbes([...prior.keys()],[...after.keys()]);
+  previous=after;
+  return {child,fresh};
+}
+const checks={parentNonRoot:false,preflightOwned:false,preflightRemoved:false,uidGidGroupsLoopbackMatched:false,
+  targetsBeforeMatched:false,targetsAfterMatched:false,journalOwnershipMatched:false,ownRootVerified:false,ownTempRemoved:false};
+try {
+  const e=process.env;
+  need(process.argv.length===2&&process.version==='v22.23.4'&&Number.isSafeInteger(uid)&&uid>0&&Number.isSafeInteger(gid)&&gid>=0);
+  need(e.GITHUB_REPOSITORY===config.repo&&e.GITHUB_EVENT_NAME==='push'&&e.GITHUB_REF==='refs/heads/'+config.branch
+    &&e.GITHUB_JOB===config.job&&e.GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT==='1'&&/^[1-9][0-9]{0,19}$/.test(e.GITHUB_RUN_ID??'')
+    &&/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(e.GITHUB_SHA??'')&&e.RUNNER_TEMP&&root===await realpath(root)
+    &&workspace===resolve(e.GITHUB_WORKSPACE??'')&&fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)===join(workspace,config.runner));
+  need(gitText('rev-parse','HEAD')===e.GITHUB_SHA&&gitText('rev-parse','HEAD^')===config.BASE&&
+    gitText('rev-parse',config.BASE+'^{tree}')===config.TREE&&gitText('show','-s','--format=%B','HEAD')===config.message);
+  const tree=gitText('rev-parse','HEAD^{tree}');need(/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(tree));
+  const files={};for(const [key,pin]of Object.entries(pins))files[key]=await pinned(workspace,pin);
+  const selection=JSON.parse(files.selection), index=JSON.parse(files.index);
+  need(digest(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(selection)))===config.selectHash&&index.entries.length===13
+    &&index.context.baselineSource===config.BASE&&index.context.baselineTree===config.TREE
+    &&index.context.repository===config.repo&&index.context.status==='SOURCE_TESTS_PENDING');
+  for(const [i,entry] of index.entries.entries())need(entry.dataPath===(i>=8&&i<=10?config.prefix:'docs/superpowers/source-lab/c2-t1/')+'body-'+String(i).padStart(2,'0')+'.txt');
+  need(JSON.stringify(index.caseMap.map(x=>x.id))===JSON.stringify(selection.selected)&&selection.selected.length===20);
+  const lock=await readFile(join(workspace,'package-lock.json'));
+  need(lock.equals(hostCmd('git',['show',config.BASE+':package-lock.json']))&&JSON.parse(lock).packages?.['node_modules/@cloudflare/workers-oauth-provider']?.version==='1.2.1');
+  adapters=await import('./collection-adapters.mjs');
+  await admitTransport();
+  context={repository:config.repo,baselineSource:config.BASE,baselineTree:config.TREE,sourceIndexBlob:pins.index.blob,
+    proposalIndexHash:pins.index.sha256,selectionHash:config.selectHash,head:e.GITHUB_SHA,tree,runId:e.GITHUB_RUN_ID,
+    runAttempt:1,job:config.job,node:process.version};
+  checks.parentNonRoot=true;
+  phase='materialize';
+  temp=await mkdtemp(join(root,'afw-green20-')); tempInitial=await lstat(temp);
+  need(dirname(temp)===root&&tempInitial.isDirectory()&&tempInitial.uid===uid&&tempInitial.gid===gid);
+  await materialize(index,files);
+  await adapters.installSiblings(target,selection,files.driver,files.recorder);
+  phase='preflight';
+  const pf=await checkedLaunch([join(target,'c2-source-custody-preflight.mjs')],10000);
+  pfCount=pf.fresh;
+  need(!pf.child.error&&pf.child.signal===null&&pf.child.status===0&&
+    pf.child.stdout?.equals(Buffer.from('AFW_C2_OAUTH_PREFLIGHT {"schema":"afw.c2-oauth-preflight.v1","written":true}\n'))&&pf.child.stderr?.length===0);
+  const pd=join(target,'preflight-owned'),probe=join(pd,'probe'),st=await lstat(probe);
+  need((await lstat(pd)).isDirectory()&&await realpath(probe)===probe&&st.isFile()&&st.nlink===1&&st.uid===uid&&st.gid===gid&&
+    st.size===15&&(await readFile(probe)).equals(Buffer.from('C2_OAUTH_OWNED\n')));
+  checks.preflightOwned=true;await rm(pd,{recursive:true,force:false});await existsAbsent(pd);checks.preflightRemoved=true;
+  phase='materialize';
+  const journalPath=join(target,'green-progress.ndjson'),initial=await adapters.createJournal(journalPath);
+  await adapters.verifySiblings(target,selection);
+  beforeHashes=await adapters.measureTargets(target,index.entries);
+  expected=index.entries.map(e=>e.after.sha256);checks.targetsBeforeMatched=true;
+  phase='green';
+  const green=await checkedLaunch(['--experimental-vm-modules','--test','--test-reporter=tap','--test-concurrency=1',
+    config.childFile],180000);greenCount=green.fresh;
+  checks.uidGidGroupsLoopbackMatched=true;
+  phase='journal';
+  await adapters.verifySiblings(target,selection);
+  afterHashes=await adapters.measureTargets(target,index.entries);checks.targetsAfterMatched=true;
+  const raw=await adapters.readJournal(journalPath,initial);checks.journalOwnershipMatched=true;
+  phase='native';observation=adapters.collectObservation(green.child,raw,selection.selected);
+} catch(error) {
+  fault={phase,reason:valid[phase].includes(error?.code)?error.code:valid[phase][0]};
+} finally {
+  if(temp) {
+    try {
+      phase='cleanup';
+      const s=await lstat(temp);
+      need(tempInitial&&dirname(temp)===root&&await realpath(temp)===temp&&s.isDirectory()&&
+        s.dev===tempInitial.dev&&s.ino===tempInitial.ino&&s.uid===uid&&s.gid===gid);
+      checks.ownRootVerified=true;await rm(temp,{recursive:true,force:false});await existsAbsent(temp);checks.ownTempRemoved=true;
+    } catch {fault={phase:'cleanup',reason:'CLEANUP_INVALID'};}
+  }
+}
+if(!fault) {
+  try {
+    phase='publish';remain(330000);
+    const custody=adapters.custodyFrame(context,pfCount,greenCount,checks);
+    const packet=adapters.successPacket(context,custody,beforeHashes,afterHashes,expected,observation);
+    const dest=join(workspace,config.artifact),outDir=dirname(dest);
+    await mkdir(outDir,{recursive:true});
+    need(await realpath(outDir)===outDir);
+    await writeFile(dest,packet.payload,{flag:'wx',mode:0o600});
+    need((await readFile(dest)).equals(Buffer.from(packet.payload))&&Date.now()<end);
+    process.stdout.write(packet.custodyLine+packet.outcomeLine);
+  } catch {fault={phase:'publish',reason:'PUBLICATION_INVALID'};}
+}
+if(fault) {
+  process.exitCode=1;
+  const text=adapters?adapters.faultLine(context,fault.phase,fault.reason):
+    'AFW_C2_DROP_WINNER_DIAG4_FAULT '+JSON.stringify({schema:'afw.c2-drop-winner-diag4-fault.v1',context,
+    phase:fault.phase,reason:fault.reason,diagnosticCollectionComplete:false,nativeQualification:false,
+    SourceQualified:false,Task1Pass:false,nonclaims:flags})+'\n';
+  process.stderr.write(text);
+}
